mwg:autofill-sign-up-form:3 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Rebrand checkboxes and radios with one line of accent-color instead of rebuilding them New capabilityStalemateLimited Brand-consistent forms · Hand-built controls lose native behavior
Unlock · brainstormBrand-colored checkboxes, sliders and progress bars used to mean rewriting the controls → surveys and settings pages can use native controls recolored with one line of CSS, with indeterminate, keyboard and focus handled by the browser. Only affects checkbox, radio, range and progress
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Set accent-color: var(--brand-color) on body or a form container so checkbox, radio, range and progress take the brand color; pair it with color-scheme for dark mode
Blocker
Building custom controls from scratch takes time and easily leads to accessibility issues or missing states such as a checkbox's indeterminate state (quote: "Building custom controls is time-consuming and can easily lead to accessibility issues or missing states (like the indeterminate state for checkboxes).")
Outcome
Stalemate — The guide marks accent-color as Limited and requires the old 'visually hidden native input plus custom drawing' approach as an @supports not fallback (official claim)

The design calls for brand-purple checkboxes, native controls only come in the browser's default blue, so the usual fix was hiding the native input and redrawing it with pseudo-elements, often forgetting indeterminate or focus styles. accent-color recolors the native controls themselves. The guide still requires keeping the redraw for unsupported browsers.

Support accent-color: Limited per the guide (which also lists Chrome 93, Edge 93, Firefox 92 and Safari 26.2 as supporting; marked Limited despite all four, pending manual check)

mwg:brand-consistent-forms · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Pick an accent-color with enough contrast yourself instead of trusting automatic inversion CorrectionPartialLimited Brand-consistent forms · Focus or state indicators disappear
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Choose a brand color with at least 3:1 contrast against the background, use a lighter value in dark mode, and pair it with color-scheme: light dark
Blocker
Safari (WebKit bug 244233) and Android Chrome (Chromium bug 343503163) can fail to invert the checkmark color, leaving controls invisible or hard to see when contrast is low (quote: "can fail to invert checkmark colors, leading to invisible or hard-to-see controls when using colors that lack sufficient contrast against the background")
Outcome
Partial — With base contrast ensured, controls stay visible even when the browser doesn't invert; the bugs remain, and the guide lists accent-color as Limited (official claim)

A pale yellow brand color in light mode should, in theory, get an automatically contrasting checkmark, but some implementations don't, and checked versus unchecked becomes nearly invisible. The guide's answer: don't bet on the browser, pick a contrasting color from the start.

Support accent-color: Limited (as above; the guide's status is questionable)

mwg:brand-consistent-forms:2 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Build branded dropdowns with appearance: base-select instead of rebuilding them in JS New capabilityPartialLimited Branded select styling · Hand-built controls lose native behavior
Unlock · brainstormA branded dropdown used to mean writing your own listbox component → every dropdown in a form can match the design system in pure CSS, with keyboard, screen reader and submission needing no extra work. Firefox falls back to the system menu
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Set appearance: base-select on <select> and ::picker(select), use a <button> containing <selectedcontent> as the trigger, and style the list, arrow and checkmark via ::picker(select), ::picker-icon and option::checkmark
Blocker
A fully branded dropdown used to require rebuilding the control from scratch in JavaScript, which often broke accessibility, keyboard navigation and native form integration (quote: "Previously, achieving a fully branded select required rebuilding the control from scratch with JavaScript, which often broke accessibility, keyboard navigation, and native form integration.")
Outcome
Partial — Customizable <select> is Limited (Chrome/Edge 135, Safari 27, not Firefox); where supported the browser handles focus, top layer and accessibility, and elsewhere it falls back to the OS dropdown, keeping function but losing the brand look (official claim)

The design system wants dropdowns with dashed borders and monospace type like every other control; native <select> couldn't do it, so teams built their own and then had to re-add arrow keys, screen reader support and form submission one by one. base-select puts the look entirely in CSS while behavior stays native. The cost: no native iOS wheel picker.

Support Customizable <select>: Limited (Chrome 135, Edge 135, Safari 27; not Firefox)

mwg:branded-select-styling · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Lay out the native select picker as a grid of cards with base-select and grid New capabilityPartialLimited Custom select picker layouts · Hand-built controls lose native behavior
Unlock · brainstormOptions used to be a column of text, and visual choices meant writing your own component → colors, sizes, stickers or plan comparisons can be a native <select> laid out as a card grid that submits with the form. Firefox falls back to text only; Left/Right navigation and grid semantics for screen readers remain limited
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Set appearance: base-select on <select> and ::picker(select), use display: grid on select:open::picker(select), put images or SVGs in <option> as cards, and mark the chosen one with option:checked and ::checkmark
Blocker
(missing) The guide only says you can break away from the traditional vertical list without custom JS, without saying when the list or custom JS goes wrong (quote: "break away from the traditional stacked vertical list and create a rich visual experience without custom JS.")
Outcome
Partial — Customizable <select> is Limited (not Firefox); unsupported browsers fall back to the system dropdown, drop non-text content in <option> and show text only; Left/Right arrow keys still need scripting (official claim)

Color swatches, emoji or product variants are easier to choose from a grid than a long text list, which used to mean abandoning <select> and building your own. The picker can now use grid layout while value and submission stay native. But native select only handles Up/Down, so a grid layout needs extra keyboard handling.

Support Customizable <select>: Limited (Chrome 135, Edge 135, Safari 27; not Firefox)

mwg:custom-select-picker-layouts · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Put picker layout styles only on :open CorrectionPartialLimited Custom select picker layouts · Side effects of a new feature
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Write layout styles like display: grid on select:open::picker(select), not directly on select::picker(select)
Blocker
Setting display directly on select::picker(select) shows the picker even while the select is closed (quote: "If you style select::picker(select), it will make the picker visible even when the select is closed!")
Outcome
Partial — With :open, the layout applies only while the picker is open; this pitfall only exists in browsers that support base-select (Limited) (official claim)

Writing ::picker(select) { display: grid } the way you would for any element, the option grid is spread across the page before the menu is ever opened. Picker layout styles must wait for the open state.

Support Customizable <select>: Limited

mwg:custom-select-picker-layouts:2 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Always wrap <selectedcontent> in a <button> CorrectionPartialLimited Custom select picker layouts · Side effects of a new feature
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
To show rich content for the selected option in the trigger, put <selectedcontent> inside the <button> within <select>
Blocker
Without a wrapping <button>, <selectedcontent> just becomes ordinary content in the picker (quote: "the <selectedcontent> will just become part of the picker, like any other content element.")
Outcome
Partial — Inside a <button>, the trigger shows the selected option's content; this pitfall only exists in browsers that support base-select (Limited) (official claim)

You want the closed dropdown to show the chosen color swatch, drop in <selectedcontent>, and it ends up in the option list while the trigger still shows text only. It only works inside the trigger <button>.

Support Customizable <select>: Limited

mwg:custom-select-picker-layouts:3 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Let inputs, textareas and selects grow and shrink with field-sizing: content New capabilityPartialNewly Form fields that automatically fit their contents · Size doesn't follow content
Unlock · brainstorm'Inputs that grow as you type' used to need JS mirroring or measuring → chat composers, inline rename fields and tag inputs can hug their text in pure CSS, looking like plain text yet editable on click
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Set field-sizing: content on <input>, <textarea> and <select>, resetting inherited global width/height to auto
Blocker
Form controls have fixed sizes by default regardless of how much the user enters, so overflow scrolls inside the field; auto-growing in older browsers needs a Grid trick with a hidden pseudo-element mirroring the input in real time (quote: "Their sizes remain constant, regardless of the amount of content the user enters or selects.")
Outcome
Partial — field-sizing is Newly available (2026-06-16); unsupported browsers fall back to fixed sizes with scrolling inside the field (official claim)

A comment box is three lines tall, and writing more means scrolling inside a tiny frame; making it grow used to mean duplicating styles and syncing text into a hidden element with JS. One property replaces that whole mirror. The guide recommends most apps simply accept fixed sizes in older browsers rather than force a fallback.

Support field-sizing: Newly (2026-06-16; Chrome 123, Edge 123, Firefox 152, Safari 26.2)

mwg:form-fields-automatically-fit-contents · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Give auto-width fields both a minimum and a maximum width CorrectionPartialNewly Form fields that automatically fit their contents · Layout unexpectedly overflows or shifts
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Set min-inline-size (e.g. 15ch) and max-inline-size (e.g. 50ch) on text inputs using field-sizing: content, and max-inline-size on <select>
Blocker
Without a minimum, an empty field collapses to zero width and can't be clicked; without a maximum, it grows indefinitely and breaks the page layout (quote: "A minimum size prevents the input from collapsing to a width of zero when empty (making it unclickable), and a maximum size ensures it doesn't expand indefinitely and break the page layout.")
Outcome
Partial — The bounds stop the field at both ends; field-sizing itself is Newly available (official claim)

An empty input with no placeholder simply vanishes once field-sizing is on; paste a long URL and the field pushes the whole row off screen. Give it a sensible range to grow within.

Support field-sizing: Newly (2026-06-16)

mwg:form-fields-automatically-fit-contents:2 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Give auto-growing textareas a fixed width CorrectionPartialNewly Form fields that automatically fit their contents · Layout unexpectedly overflows or shifts
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
When a <textarea> uses field-sizing: content, set an explicit width (width: 50ch or 100%) so only height follows content, plus min-block-size: 3lh and max-block-size: 10lh
Blocker
If a textarea can auto-size horizontally, one with a long placeholder abruptly shrinks when the user types the first character (quote: "a textarea with a long placeholder will abruptly shrink horizontally when the user types a single character")
Outcome
Partial — With a fixed width only the height grows as text wraps, so it doesn't jump sideways while typing; field-sizing itself is Newly available (official claim)

A full-sentence placeholder stretches the textarea that wide; the moment the user starts typing, the placeholder disappears and the box snaps to one character wide. Pin the width and let it grow downward until a scrollbar appears at the cap.

Support field-sizing: Newly (2026-06-16)

mwg:form-fields-automatically-fit-contents:3 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Explicitly mark helper buttons type="button" so they don't submit the form CorrectionWinN/A Forms · Forms submit unintentionally
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Set type="button" on secondary or helper buttons in a form (show password, add row, clear); keep the main submit button as type="submit"
Blocker
A <button> inside <form> defaults to type="submit", so a helper button without a type submits the form when pressed (quote: "explicitly set type="button" on secondary or helper buttons so they don't default to type="submit".")
Outcome
Win — Marked as button, helpers only do their own job and don't trigger submission (official claim)

Halfway through a form, the user presses a helper like 'Show password' and the whole form submits, just because that button had no type and fell back to submit. The opposite applies to the main submit button: don't make it type="button".

Support N/A (the guide has no browser support data at all)

mwg:forms · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Send sensitive data with POST, not GET CorrectionWinN/A Forms · Sensitive data leaks
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Use method="POST" for sensitive data and state-changing forms; reserve GET for idempotent requests like search
Blocker
Sending sensitive data with GET exposes it in browser history and server logs (quote: "DON'T use GET for sensitive data (it exposes data in history/logs).")
Outcome
Win — With POST the data goes in the request body, not the URL (official claim)

GET appends form fields to the URL, and URLs persist in browser history and in server and proxy access logs. Fine for a search query, not for ID numbers or passwords.

Support N/A (the guide has no browser support data at all)

mwg:forms:2 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Use a checkbox, not a radio, for a single yes/no choice CorrectionWinN/A Forms · Wrong native control for the job
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Pick controls by option count: a checkbox for a single binary toggle, radios for 2–5 exclusive options, <select> only for 6+, <datalist> for large or dynamic lists
Blocker
A single radio button can't be unchecked once checked (quote: "Can be checked and unchecked with space unlike a radio button, which gets stuck once checked.")
Outcome
Win — A checkbox can be checked and unchecked with Space, so the state is reversible (official claim)

'Subscribe to the newsletter' built as a single radio: the user clicks it, changes their mind, and has no way to undo it. The wrong control brings the wrong behavior. The guide maps each situation to a suitable native element with an option-count table.

Support N/A (the guide has no browser support data at all)

mwg:forms:3 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Check isComposing (plus keyCode 229) before Enter-to-submit so you don't send mid-IME composition CorrectionPartialLimited IME-safe Enter to submit · Forms submit unintentionally
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
In a custom Enter keydown handler, return early without calling form.requestSubmit() when event.isComposing or event.keyCode === 229; applies to <textarea> and single-line <input> with custom Enter handling
Blocker
When typing Chinese, Japanese or Korean with an IME, Enter confirms the conversion candidate; a script that submits on Enter sends the message mid-conversion (quote: "the user's message is sent while they are still converting characters, resulting in incomplete, fragmented, or incorrect messages.")
Outcome
Partial — isComposing is Limited (not Safari, where an event-ordering bug also reset it to false by keydown time); adding the keyCode === 229 fallback reliably blocks it (official claim)

In a chat box you type a word, press Enter to confirm the conversion, and half-converted kana or phonetic input gets sent. Native implicit submission from an <input> is already handled by the browser; it's custom keydown handlers that break. The guide also warns that migrating old keyCode === 13 checks to key === 'Enter' without these two checks introduces the bug.

Support api.KeyboardEvent.isComposing: Limited (Chrome 56, Edge 79, Firefox 31; not Safari)

mwg:ime-safe-enter-submit · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Call preventDefault() in custom Enter-to-submit to avoid submitting twice CorrectionWinN/A IME-safe Enter to submit · Forms submit unintentionally
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Call event.preventDefault() first in the Enter keydown handler, then call requestSubmit() yourself
Blocker
Enter's default action inserts a newline in a <textarea> and triggers implicit submission for an <input> in a <form>, so with your own submit it goes out twice (quote: "in an <input> inside a <form> it triggers the browser's implicit submission, which would submit a second time.")
Outcome
Win — With the default action blocked, submission happens exactly once from your handler (official claim)

Search bars, inline rename fields and command palettes often handle Enter themselves; forget to block the default and the browser submits the form again. preventDefault() also stops a textarea from adding a stray newline before sending.

Support N/A (no support data for preventDefault() in the guide; its only support data is for isComposing)

mwg:ime-safe-enter-submit:2 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Flag required-field errors with :user-invalid, not in red on page load CorrectionWinWidely Required field feedback · Announcements mistimed or unreliable
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Add required to fields and trigger error styles and error text with :user-invalid, starting messages with an icon whose text alternative is 'Error' so color isn't the only cue
Blocker
Marking required fields as errors as soon as the page loads is confusing; a field should only look invalid after the user tried and failed (quote: "Marking required fields with an error state immediately upon page load can be confusing.")
Outcome
Win — :user-invalid is Widely available and only matches after the user interacts and leaves the field empty, or tries to submit, so no more onBlur handlers adding a touched class (official claim)

The form opens with a red border on every required field, telling users they've done something wrong before they've started. :user-invalid follows the browser's interaction timing so errors only show when they should. ARIA state still needs a small sync script.

Support :user-valid/:user-invalid: Widely (2023-11-02)

mwg:required-field-feedback · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Sync aria-invalid from :user-invalid, not checkValidity() CorrectionWinWidely Required field feedback · Announcements mistimed or unreliable
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Start fields at aria-invalid="false" and set "true" on blur or failed submit based on el.matches(':user-invalid')
Blocker
checkValidity() is already false when a user merely tabs through an untouched field, so assistive tech is told about errors too early (quote: "deriving it from :user-invalid, not checkValidity(), which is already false when a user tabs through an untouched field.")
Outcome
Win — Using :user-invalid as the source makes screen reader error timing match the screen (official claim)

The red border appears only after interaction, but if the ARIA sync script uses checkValidity(), a screen reader user hears 'invalid' on every field they tab through. Both sides need the same criterion. Firefox and Safari also expose empty required fields as invalid from load, so the starting value must be an explicit "false".

Support :user-valid/:user-invalid: Widely (2023-11-02)

mwg:required-field-feedback:2 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Attach the error message to aria-describedby only while the field is invalid CorrectionWinWidely Required field feedback · Announcements mistimed or unreliable
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Point the field's data-error at the error message id and have the script list it in aria-describedby only while the field is invalid
Blocker
Referencing the error message in the markup makes the hidden message get read from page load (quote: "A reference in the markup would read the hidden message from page load.")
Outcome
Win — The message is linked only while the field is invalid, so the screen reader reads it with the field on focus (official claim)

The error element sits in the page, hidden with CSS, with the description already wired up, so the first focus reads 'This field is required.' Attaching it only when invalid means users hear the current state.

Support :user-valid/:user-invalid: Widely (2023-11-02)

mwg:required-field-feedback:3 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

用 appearance: base-select 讓 option 放圖示與說明，擋掉「只能純文字、只好自製下拉」 New capabilityuntranslated 富媒體選擇器（可自訂的 select） · Hand-built controls lose native behavior
Unlock · brainstorm帶頭像的指派人選單、帶色票的標籤選單、帶圖示與一行說明的方案選擇，都能用原生 <select> 做，直接參與表單送出，不必再載下拉元件庫。目前 Firefox 退回純文字
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
<select> 與 ::picker(select) 都設 appearance: base-select，在 <option> 裡放 SVG、圖片、標題與說明，按鈕內用 <button><selectedcontent> 自動映出選中內容
Blocker
原生 <option> 裡的標記會被瀏覽器剝掉，只剩純文字，想要圖示加說明的選項只能改用沉重的自製元件（引用："Prior to this API, the browser would strip tags from <option> tags and render only plain text."）
Outcome
部分改善——支援的瀏覽器裡 option 可放任意 HTML，鍵盤操作與表單送出照舊是原生的；Firefox 退回系統下拉，只顯示文字（官方宣稱）

角色、方案、帳號這類選項需要圖示和一行說明，原生 select 一律剝成純文字，於是大家改用 JS 框架做的假下拉。base-select 讓 option 直接裝 HTML，picker 在 top layer、用錨點定位對齊按鈕。不支援的瀏覽器仍是可用的原生下拉，只是失去圖文版面。

Support Customizable <select>：Limited availability。Supported by: Chrome 135, Edge 135, Safari 27；Unsupported in: Firefox

mwg:rich-media-picker · Upstream guide · Line file ·

option 文字要能獨立讀懂，擋掉退回時黏成一串的選項 Correctionuntranslated 富媒體選擇器（可自訂的 select） · Semantic structure misrepresents the page
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
在 option 的標題後補上視覺隱藏的句點與「Responsibilities include:」這類銜接文字，讓整段文字脫離版面也通順
Blocker

Outcome

圖文選項在 Chrome 看起來分層清楚，到 Firefox 變成「Frontend Developer React, Vue, CSS」一串，讀屏也這樣唸。補上視覺隱藏的標點與銜接詞，無論支不支援，文字內容都說得通。

mwg:rich-media-picker:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

不要在 option 上加 aria-label 覆寫文字 Correctionuntranslated 富媒體選擇器（可自訂的 select） · 可及名稱蓋掉可見文字
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
不在 <option> 上用 aria-label 取代內容，而是讓 option 的完整文字本身就能獨立讀懂
Blocker
aria-label 會覆寫 option 的文字，沒寫進 label 的可見文字，讀屏使用者就聽不到；日後改了選項文字也容易漏改（引用："This will result in people missing visible text that hasn't been included in the aria-label."）

為了讓富內容的選項唸起來短一點，在 option 上加 aria-label，結果畫面上的說明文字讀屏全聽不到，兩邊內容各自漂移。指南改要求文字本身寫通順，不另外造一個名字。

<option> 裡只放文字、圖示等靜態內容，不放連結、按鈕或帶互動語意的元素
Blocker

mwg:rich-media-picker:4 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

base-select 不再依最長選項定寬，要自己給寬度 Correctionuntranslated 富媒體選擇器（可自訂的 select） · Side effects of a new feature
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
開啟 appearance: base-select 後，替 select 設固定寬度或用 flex／grid 限制尺寸
Blocker
base-select 拿掉了瀏覽器依最長選項決定 select 寬度的預設行為，選項一換寬度就跟著變，造成版面位移（引用："Setting appearance: base-select removes the default browser behavior of sizing the select based on its longest option width."）
Outcome
部分改善——給定寬度後版面穩定；這是開啟新功能後才需要補的一步（官方宣稱）

原生 select 會預先量最長的選項，所以選什麼寬度都不變。換成 base-select 後按鈕寬度跟著選中內容走，選一個長名字整排表單就被推開。補一個寬度限制就好。

Support Customizable <select>：Limited availability（同上）

mwg:rich-media-picker:5 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 select:user-invalid 讓必選下拉等互動後才標錯 Correctionuntranslated 下拉選單的互動後驗證 · Announcements mistimed or unreliable
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
<select required> 的第一個選項設 value="" 並停用，錯誤框線與訊息用 select:user-invalid 觸發，成功用 :user-valid
Blocker
必選下拉的預設選項是空值時，標準驗證一載入就把欄位標成無效，製造視覺雜訊（引用："standard validation flags the field as invalid immediately if the default option has an empty value. This can create visual noise."）
Outcome
勝——:user-invalid 已 Widely；只是載入或聚焦又離開不算互動，欄位維持中性，直到使用者改過值再離開或嘗試送出（官方宣稱）

「選擇國家」這類必選下拉，空的預設選項讓它從一開始就是 :invalid，表單還沒填就一片紅。改用 :user-invalid 後，錯誤只在使用者動過又沒選、或按送出時出現。部分瀏覽器把「打開又關上」也算互動，行動裝置上的 blur 時機也不同，要實機測。

Support :user-valid／:user-invalid：Widely（2023-11-02）

mwg:select-menu-interaction · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 progress() 與 attr() 在 CSS 裡算出滑桿的填色比例 New capabilityuntranslated 品牌一致的範圍滑桿 · JS patches in styling state
Unlock · brainstorm任何「依數值填色」的原生控制項（音量、評分門檻、預算分配條）都能把比例計算留在 CSS，換 min／max 只改 HTML 屬性。目前 Safari 不支援 typed attr()，要靠 JS 補
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
在 input[type="range"] 上用 attr(min type(<number>))、attr(max …) 讀屬性，progress(var(--control-value), min, max) 算出百分比，餵給 WebKit 軌道的漸層；Firefox 用 ::-moz-range-progress
Blocker
WebKit／Blink 沒有對應「已填部分」的偽元素，要讓軌道已填和未填兩段不同色，只能自己同步數值（引用："WebKit/Blink do not provide an equivalent pseudo-element, so sync the live input value to --control-value on input events"）
Outcome
部分改善——min／max 由 CSS 從屬性讀出、比例由 progress() 計算，腳本只剩一行同步目前值；attr() 不支援時（Safari）連 min／max 也要由 JS 同步，progress() 不支援時改用 calc()（官方宣稱）

品牌滑桿要左邊填色、右邊灰色，Firefox 有偽元素，Chrome 和 Safari 沒有，只能把數值丟進自訂屬性再畫漸層。typed attr() 和 progress() 把比例計算搬進 CSS，JS 只負責回報目前值。Safari 尚不支援 typed attr()，仍要退回整套 JS 同步。

Support progress()：Newly（2026-09-01）；attr()：Limited availability。Supported by: Chrome 133, Edge 133, Firefox 155；Unsupported in: Safari

mwg:slider · Upstream guide · Line file ·

WebKit 與 Firefox 的滑桿偽元素分開寫，不要放同一個選擇器清單 Correctionuntranslated 品牌一致的範圍滑桿 · 不認得的語法讓整條規則失效
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
::-webkit-slider-thumb 與 ::-moz-range-thumb（以及 track）各寫一個規則區塊
Blocker
把兩家前綴偽元素用逗號放在同一條規則，瀏覽器遇到不認得的那個會丟掉整條規則（引用："browsers drop the entire rule if an unrecognized vendor pseudo-element is encountered."）
Outcome
勝——分開寫後，每個引擎只忽略自己不認得的那一塊，樣式在兩邊都生效（官方宣稱）

為了少寫幾行把 thumb 樣式合併成一條，結果 Chrome 不認得 -moz-、Firefox 不認得 -webkit-，兩邊都整條失效，滑桿退回系統樣式。拆成兩個區塊就各自生效。

mwg:slider:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 WebOTP 與綁網域的簡訊格式自動帶入驗證碼，擋掉切 app 抄碼 New capabilityuntranslated 簡訊一次性密碼（OTP）表單驗證 · Autofill fails
Unlock · brainstorm手機號碼驗證、付款確認這類步驟可以做到「簡訊一到就自動帶入並送出」，而且驗證碼綁網域，釣魚頁拿不到自動帶入。跨來源 iframe（3-D Secure）也能用，但目前 Permissions-Policy 委派僅 Chromium
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
單一 <input autocomplete="one-time-code" inputmode="numeric">，簡訊最後一行寫 @網域 #驗證碼，支援時用 navigator.credentials.get({ otp: { transport: ['sms'] } }) 取碼並 requestSubmit()，以 AbortController 管理每個離開路徑
Blocker
使用者要在瀏覽器和簡訊 app 之間切換、記住或複製多位數驗證碼，增加摩擦與流失，還容易在仿冒網域上被釣魚（引用："Switching between a browser and an SMS messaging app to memorize or copy-paste a multi-digit code introduces user friction, increases drop-off rates, and leaves users vulnerable to phishing on spoofed domains."）
Outcome
部分改善——支援 WebOTP 的瀏覽器先核對網域再帶入驗證碼，一次點擊完成；不支援時退回鍵盤列建議（Safari 等）或手動輸入數字鍵盤，網域綁定仍在但少了自動送出（官方宣稱）

收到簡訊、切出去、記下六位數、切回來、打錯一位重來，流程在這裡掉人；仿冒網站也一樣能叫人抄碼。網域綁定的簡訊格式讓瀏覽器與系統只在網域相符時才提供驗證碼，WebOTP 再把「貼上並送出」變成一下點擊。Firefox 沒有 WebOTP，只剩手動輸入。

Support WebOTP：Limited availability。Supported by: Chrome 93, Edge 93, Safari 27；Unsupported in: Firefox

mwg:sms-otp-form · Upstream guide · Line file ·

驗證碼用 type="text" 加 inputmode="numeric"，不要用 type="number" Correctionuntranslated 簡訊一次性密碼（OTP）表單驗證 · Wrong native control for the job
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
OTP 欄位用 type="text" 配 inputmode="numeric"、pattern="\d{6}"，不用 type="number" 也不用 type="tel"
Blocker
驗證碼是識別字串不是數量，type="number" 會多出增減按鈕、可能吃掉開頭的 0，還會被滾輪意外改值（引用："Using type="number" adds increment/decrement spinner buttons, can strip leading zeros, and allows accidental scroll-wheel value changes."）
Outcome
勝——inputmode 已 Widely，行動裝置照樣出數字鍵盤，值保持原樣的字串（官方宣稱）

為了在手機上叫出數字鍵盤而用 type="number"，結果 012345 變成 12345，捲動頁面時滑鼠滾過欄位還會把碼改掉。改用文字欄位加 inputmode，鍵盤一樣，值不會被當成數字處理。

Support inputmode：Widely（2021-12-07）

mwg:sms-otp-form:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

驗證碼用單一欄位，不要拆成六個格子 Correctionuntranslated 簡訊一次性密碼（OTP）表單驗證 · Autofill fails
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
只用一個 <input>，要分格的外觀就用等寬字型加 letter-spacing
Blocker
六個 maxlength="1" 的分格輸入，在許多瀏覽器會讓 autocomplete="one-time-code" 自動帶入失效，也妨礙貼上、讓讀屏導覽變複雜（引用："Multi-box OTP patterns (six separate maxlength="1" inputs) break native autocomplete="one-time-code" autofill in many browsers, hinder copy-pasting, and complicate screen reader navigation."）
Outcome
勝——單一欄位能吃到系統的驗證碼建議與整段貼上，外觀仍可做成分格（官方宣稱）

設計稿畫了六個方格，實作成六個輸入框，系統跳出的驗證碼建議卻填不進去，貼上也只進第一格。換成一個欄位加字距，看起來一樣，自動帶入和貼上都恢復。

Support inputmode：Widely（2021-12-07）；指南未附 autocomplete="one-time-code" 的支援資料

mwg:sms-otp-form:3 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

自動取碼後用 requestSubmit()，不要用 form.submit() Correctionuntranslated 簡訊一次性密碼（OTP）表單驗證 · Forms submit unintentionally
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
WebOTP 取到碼、填進欄位後呼叫 form.requestSubmit()（或直接呼叫自己的驗證函式）
Blocker
form.submit() 會繞過 submit 事件監聽（preventDefault()／fetch() 處理）並跳過原生約束驗證（引用："form.submit() bypasses submit event listeners (event.preventDefault() / fetch() handlers) and skips native constraint validation."）
Outcome
勝——requestSubmit() 走和使用者按送出一樣的路徑，監聽器與驗證都會執行（官方宣稱）

自動取碼後順手 form.submit()，頁面直接以原生 POST 跳走，原本寫在 submit 事件裡的 fetch 送出、中止監聽、格式驗證全被略過。改成 requestSubmit() 就和手動按下送出鈕一致。

Support 不適用（指南未附 requestSubmit() 的支援資料）

格式提示放在欄位上方，擋掉被自動填入浮層和鍵盤蓋住 Correctionuntranslated 簡訊一次性密碼（OTP）表單驗證 · 浮層遮住說明
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
可見 <label> 之外，格式說明（以 aria-describedby 連結）放在 <input> 的視覺上方
Blocker

「請輸入寄到手機的 6 位數驗證碼」放在欄位下面，一點欄位鍵盤升起、驗證碼建議跳出，說明正好被蓋掉。挪到上方就一直看得到。

mwg:sms-otp-form:5 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 <input type="checkbox" switch> 做原生開關 New capabilityuntranslated 無障礙的開關控制項 · Hand-built controls lose native behavior
Unlock · brainstorm設定頁的每個開關都是一行 HTML，表單送出、鍵盤、讀屏「開／關」語意都由瀏覽器處理，不必維護開關元件。目前僅 Safari 原生，其他瀏覽器靠 polyfill
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
在一般 checkbox 上加布林屬性 switch，配 <label>，用 accent-color 換色、:focus-visible 補焦點框；不支援時依 'switch' in HTMLInputElement.prototype 有條件載入 input-switch-polyfill
Blocker
缺——指南只說 switch 保住原生表單送出、鍵盤操作與讀屏語意，沒說自製開關在哪個時刻出錯
Outcome
僵持——只有 Safari 17.4 起支援，Chrome、Edge、Firefox 都要靠 polyfill（補上 role="switch"、開關外觀與拖曳）（官方宣稱）

開關本質就是 checkbox，但長久以來只能自己畫。switch 屬性讓瀏覽器直接畫出開關並暴露 switch 角色，送出值和 checkbox 一樣。目前只有 Safari 原生支援，其他瀏覽器都得載 polyfill。

Support <input type="checkbox" switch>：Limited availability。Supported by: Safari 17.4；Unsupported in: Chrome, Edge, Firefox

mwg:switch · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 :user-invalid 等使用者打完再報錯，擋掉邊打邊變紅 Correctionuntranslated 互動後才驗證輸入 · Announcements mistimed or unreliable
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
用 type="email"、pattern、required 宣告規則，錯誤樣式與錯誤文字用 :user-invalid 觸發，:user-valid 給成功提示
Blocker
使用者一聚焦開始打字就跳錯誤，例如 email 打到「user@gm」時技術上無效，標準 :invalid 立刻顯示錯誤狀態（引用："Standard :invalid styling results in an error state appearing immediately, frustrating the user."）
Outcome
勝——:user-invalid 已 Widely，錯誤延到使用者改過值並離開、或嘗試送出時才出現，改正後立刻消失（官方宣稱）

密碼規則五條，打第一個字就整欄變紅，使用者還沒打完就被告知做錯。:user-invalid 照「改過並離開」的時機判斷，打字途中保持中性，離開時才給回饋，修好當下錯誤就消失。

Support :user-valid／:user-invalid：Widely（2023-11-02）

mwg:validate-input-after-interaction · Upstream guide · Line file ·

規則清單滿足後用 visibility: hidden 隱藏，不要收掉空間 Correctionuntranslated 互動後才驗證輸入 · Layout unexpectedly overflows or shifts
Who acts

Action + object

mwg:validate-input-after-interaction:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

只把規則清單染紅，沒說哪裡錯，讀屏也不會傳達顏色變化（引用："Turning the rules list red doesn't say what is wrong, and screen readers don't convey the color change."）

mwg:validate-input-after-interaction:4 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

fallback 選擇器用 :is() 包起來，擋掉一個不認得就整條失效 Correctionuntranslated 互動後才驗證輸入 · 不認得的語法讓整條規則失效

開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object

mwg:validate-input-after-interaction:5 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 ariaLabelledByElements 等元素參照跨過 shadow 邊界，擋掉 id 參照默默失效 New capabilityuntranslated 無障礙的 Web Components · 封裝邊界切斷關聯
Unlock · brainstorm設計系統的輸入元件可以讓使用端把標籤、說明、錯誤訊息寫在自己的頁面裡，元件內部的原生控制項照樣被正確標示，不必把整段說明搬進 shadow 或改用 aria-label
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
shadow tree 內的控制項用 ariaLabelledByElements、ariaDescribedByElements 這類反射 ARIA 元素屬性，直接拿 Light DOM（或 slot 進來的內容）的元素當標籤或描述
Blocker
aria-labelledby、aria-describedby 這類 id 參照只在自己那棵樹裡解析，Light DOM 指不到 shadow 裡的 id、反之亦然，參照會被默默忽略（引用："An id-based attribute in the Light DOM cannot point at an id inside a shadow tree, and vice versa; the reference is silently ignored."）
Outcome
部分改善——元素參照可以「向外」解析，從 shadow 內指向 Light DOM 或任何祖先樹，id 做不到的關聯現在表達得出來；「向內」（宿主被自己的 shadow 內容標示）仍不行，Reference Target 尚未普及；不支援時只能退回 aria-label 文字（官方宣稱）

元件把真正的 <input> 包在 shadow 裡，頁面上的說明文字卻在 Light DOM，aria-describedby 寫了也沒有作用，讀屏聽不到說明，而且沒有任何錯誤訊息。元素參照屬性讓 shadow 內的控制項直接指向外面的節點。反方向仍是空白。

Support Element.ariaLabelledByElements：Newly（2025-04-04）。Supported by: Chrome 135, Edge 135, Firefox 136, Safari 16.4

mwg:accessible-web-components · Upstream guide · Line file ·

宿主自帶角色時不要開 delegatesFocus Correctionuntranslated 無障礙的 Web Components · Focus or state indicators disappear
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
宿主本身就是控制項（以 ElementInternals 設角色與狀態）時，在 connectedCallback 裡把宿主放進 Tab 順序；只有包住內部原生控制項時才用 delegatesFocus: true
Blocker
delegatesFocus: true 與宿主上的角色／狀態同時使用，焦點會落到內部元素，略過宿主的狀態（引用："Do not combine delegatesFocus: true with a role/state on the host, or focus will land on the inner element and skip the host's state."）
Outcome
勝——焦點落在帶角色的宿主上，輔助技術會唸出它的角色與狀態（官方宣稱）

開關元件在宿主上宣告 role="switch" 與 aria-checked，又順手開了 delegatesFocus，Tab 進去焦點跑到內部某個元素，讀屏聽不到「開／關」。兩種模式擇一。

Support ARIA attribute reflection：Widely（2023-10-24）

connectedCallback 裡 if (!this.hasAttribute('tabindex')) this.tabIndex = 0

mwg:accessible-web-components:3 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 connectedMoveCallback 接住 moveBefore() 的搬移，擋掉搬一次就重來 New capabilityuntranslated 自訂元素 · Rebuilding the DOM loses state
Unlock · brainstorm包著播放器、地圖、編輯器的自訂元素可以在版面間自由搬動（拖到側欄、彈出成浮動視窗再放回）而不重新載入，可重排的工作區元件真正可行。支援度依 moveBefore()，見 mwg:drag-and-drop
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
自訂元素定義 connectedMoveCallback，元素被 Node.moveBefore() 搬動時改跑這個回呼，不跑 disconnect／connect
Blocker
一般搬動會先 disconnectedCallback 再 connectedCallback，昂貴或看得出來的 setup／teardown 整套重跑；connectedMoveCallback 存在的理由就是讓狀態、焦點、iframe／媒體播放撐過搬移（引用："this callback fires *instead of* disconnectedCallback → connectedCallback, so state, focus, and iframe/media playback survive the move."）
Outcome
勝——用 moveBefore() 搬動時不再拆掉重建，狀態、焦點、播放都保留（官方宣稱）

元件裡有影片或 iframe，拖到別的欄位就整個重新初始化，播放中斷、焦點掉了。moveBefore() 加上 connectedMoveCallback 讓搬移成為一件獨立的事，元件可以只更新位置相關的東西。

Support 不適用（本指南未附支援資料；moveBefore() 在 mwg:drag-and-drop 記為 Limited）

mwg:custom-elements · Upstream guide · Line file ·

connectedCallback 要能重跑，teardown 寫在 disconnectedCallback Correctionuntranslated 自訂元素 · Wrong lifecycle assumptions
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
connectedCallback 的 setup 寫成冪等；全域監聽、計時器、observer 在 disconnectedCallback 移除，但不當成永久銷毀
Blocker
connectedCallback 每次插入都會觸發，搬動元素就再跑一次；不在 disconnect 時清掉全域監聽與計時器就會洩漏（引用："It fires on every insertion, so moving the element re-runs it."）
Outcome
勝——冪等的 setup 配上對稱的 teardown，元素被搬動或重新插入時不會重複掛監聽，也不會洩漏（官方宣稱）

把 connectedCallback 當成「只跑一次的初始化」，元素一被移動就再掛一次監聽，事件觸發兩遍；拿掉時沒清計時器，背景持續跑。指南把它當成「每次接上都會跑」來設計。

Support 不適用（本指南未附支援資料）

mwg:custom-elements:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

生命週期回呼與 observedAttributes 要在 define() 之前就寫好 Correctionuntranslated 自訂元素 · Wrong lifecycle assumptions
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
static observedAttributes 與所有生命週期方法在呼叫 customElements.define() 前就放在類別上
Blocker
define() 只在註冊時讀一次這些設定，之後才加上的回呼或屬性清單會被默默忽略，attributeChangedCallback 永遠不觸發（引用："attributeChangedCallback never fires unless observedAttributes was already present at define() time."）
Outcome
勝——註冊前就定義完整，屬性變化與生命週期都會如預期觸發（官方宣稱）

先 define()，之後才在類別上補 observedAttributes 或掛回呼，改屬性時元件毫無反應，也沒有錯誤可查。註冊是一次快照，要在那之前定型。

Support 不適用（本指南未附支援資料）

mwg:custom-elements:3 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

升級時屬性可能已在、子節點可能還沒到，用 slotchange 與 attributeChangedCallback 補 Correctionuntranslated 自訂元素 · Wrong lifecycle assumptions
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
constructor 先呼叫 super()；在 connectedCallback 讀既有屬性同步初始狀態；用 slotchange 監看子節點、attributeChangedCallback 監看屬性，不忽略之後的觸發
Blocker
元素可能在類別註冊前就已在 DOM 裡，升級時屬性和子節點早就存在；反過來，長清單的子節點在 connectedCallback 時可能還沒解析完（引用："the HTML parser can yield mid-parse of long child lists, so the callback sometimes runs before every child is attached."）；在 super() 之前碰 this 會丟 ReferenceError
Outcome
勝——以事件追蹤後續到達的屬性與子節點，不論元素何時升級都能正確初始化（官方宣稱）

伺服器輸出的元素在腳本晚載時才升級，元件假設自己是空的、把既有內容蓋掉；或在 connectedCallback 數子節點，偶爾只數到一半。改成「升級時讀現況、之後靠事件補」就不受時序影響。

Support 不適用（本指南未附支援資料）

mwg:custom-elements:4 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用延遲的 opacity 動畫隱藏未定義元素，不要用 visibility: hidden 硬藏 Correctionuntranslated 自訂元素 · Hiding or disabling locks people out
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
my-element:not(:defined) 設 opacity: 0 並加一個 2 秒後轉為不透明的動畫；只對需要的元素或子樹（:has(:not(:defined))）設，不對整個 body
Blocker
硬藏未升級的元素時，腳本一旦沒載入或載得慢，內容就永遠看不到；把守衛設在整個 body，一個不重要的小工具就會拖住整頁繪製（引用："so a failed or slow script never hides content for good."）
Outcome
勝——「失敗時打開」，避免未樣式化的閃爍，腳本失敗時內容也會在逾時後出現（官方宣稱）

為了避免元件升級前的閃爍，在 :not(:defined) 寫 visibility: hidden，結果分享按鈕的腳本被擋掉時整塊內容永遠空白。改成延遲淡入，最壞情況只是晚兩秒出現。

Support 不適用（本指南未附支援資料）

mwg:custom-elements:5 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 formAssociated 與 ElementInternals 讓自訂控制項自己送值，不要藏一個 input Correctionuntranslated 表單關聯自訂元素 · Hand-built controls lose native behavior
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
類別加 static formAssociated = true，用 setFormValue() 送值、setValidity() 接上原生驗證，並實作 formResetCallback、formDisabledCallback、formStateRestoreCallback
Blocker
缺——指南只說不要退回隱藏的 <input>，列出 FACE 的好處（自己送值、參與驗證、反映表單狀態），沒說隱藏 input 在哪個時刻出錯
Outcome
勝——FACE 已 Widely；元素自己送出值，無效時表單不會送出，:invalid／:valid 生效，reset、fieldset[disabled]、上一頁還原都像原生控制項（官方宣稱）

自訂的評分元件要參與表單，傳統做法是旁邊藏一個 input 同步值。FACE 讓元素本身就是表單控制項，送值、驗證、重設、停用都走瀏覽器的路。指南也提醒，:user-invalid 在 FACE 上各瀏覽器還沒一致接好。

Support Form-associated custom elements：Widely（2023-03-27）

mwg:form-associated-custom-elements · Upstream guide · Line file ·

formDisabledCallback 只停用內部控制項，不要設宿主的 disabled Correctionuntranslated 表單關聯自訂元素 · Hiding or disabling locks people out
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
在 formDisabledCallback(disabled) 裡停用 shadow 內的按鈕（或移出 Tab 順序並忽略輸入），宿主本身不碰
Blocker
在回呼裡替宿主加上 disabled 屬性，祖先 <fieldset> 重新啟用時宿主仍卡著自己的 disabled，永遠停用（引用："Do NOT toggle disabled on this, or a <fieldset disabled> toggle will leave the host permanently stuck with its own disabled attribute."）
Outcome
勝——只停用內部控制項，fieldset 切換時能正常恢復，宿主也會自動符合 :disabled（官方宣稱）

整個 fieldset 停用再啟用後，其他欄位都恢復了，只有自訂元件還是灰的、點不動。因為回呼把 disabled 寫到宿主上，那個屬性沒有人會拿掉。

Support Form-associated custom elements：Widely（2023-03-27）

mwg:form-associated-custom-elements:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

不要用 ElementInternals 直接給可及名稱 Correctionuntranslated 表單關聯自訂元素 · 可及名稱蓋掉可見文字
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
處理互動或作為群組的自訂元素用 ElementInternals 設角色（例如 role = 'group'）與狀態，但除非元素自帶可見標籤，不透過它設可及名稱
Blocker
用 ElementInternals 設名稱，可能蓋掉使用端給的名稱，例如用 <label for> 標示的名稱（引用："this would likely override any accessible name set when using the custom element, such as by labelling the element using a HTML label's for attribute."）
Outcome
勝——不預設名稱，使用端的 <label> 就是讀屏聽到的名稱（官方宣稱）

元件內建一個「Rating」名稱，頁面上明明用 <label for> 標成「這次服務的滿意度」，讀屏卻唸出元件預設的那個。名稱留給使用端給。

Support Form-associated custom elements：Widely（2023-03-27）

mwg:form-associated-custom-elements:3 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 <dialog> 的 showModal() 與 closedby="any" 做模態框，不自己寫焦點陷阱與關閉 Correctionuntranslated HTML · Hand-written focus management
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
模態覆蓋層用 <dialog> 加 .showModal()，closedby="any" 開啟點背景關閉，<form method="dialog"> 關閉並回傳 returnValue，::backdrop 畫背景；非模態的選單與提示用 popover
Blocker
缺——指南只說 <dialog> 會自動困住焦點、暗化背景、支援 Esc，closedby="any" 不必自訂 JS，沒說手寫版在哪個時刻出錯；只提醒需要焦點陷阱時別用 show()
Outcome
勝——焦點陷阱、Esc、點背景關閉、回傳值都由瀏覽器處理（官方宣稱）

模態框是最常被手寫的元件之一：攔 Tab、攔 Esc、算點擊是否在外面。<dialog> 加 showModal() 把這些都收進平台，closedby 連點背景關閉也宣告式了。仍要提供看得見的關閉按鈕，讓語音控制的使用者知道怎麼離開。

Support 不適用（本指南未附支援資料）

mwg:html · Upstream guide · Line file ·

Who acts

抽屜打開後，背後的導覽連結仍在 Tab 順序裡，鍵盤使用者一路按會跑到看不見、也不該操作的地方。inert 一個屬性把整塊關掉，不必逐一改 tabindex。

mwg:html:6 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

Blocker

mwg:html:9 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 Declarative Shadow DOM 從伺服器送出 shadow tree，擋掉等 JS 才有樣式的閃爍 Correctionuntranslated 預先渲染自訂元素 · Content takes shape only after JS runs
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
宿主的第一個子節點放 <template shadowrootmode="open">，元件 CSS 寫在模板內的 <style> 或 <link>，解析器直接掛上 shadow root
Blocker
shadow tree 靠腳本建立時，元件在 JS 執行前沒有結構也沒有樣式，出現未樣式化內容的閃爍（FOUC）；樣式若放在另外由腳本載入的樣式表，第一次繪製也一樣沒樣式（引用："DSD renders correctly on first paint only if styles ship inside the declarative template."）
Outcome
勝——DSD 已 Widely，元件在任何 JS 執行前就有結構與樣式，伺服器渲染與串流都適用（官方宣稱）

卡片元件的外框、間距都寫在 shadow 裡，頁面先出現一堆沒排版的文字，等元件腳本跑完才突然成形。DSD 讓解析器直接長出 shadow root，樣式隨模板送達，第一次繪製就是完成的樣子。

Support Declarative shadow DOM：Widely（2024-02-20）

mwg:prerendering-custom-elements · Upstream guide · Line file ·

hydrate 時沿用伺服器的 shadow root，不要重新渲染 Correctionuntranslated 預先渲染自訂元素 · Rebuilding the DOM loses state
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
constructor 先檢查 this.shadowRoot，已存在就沿用，只在沒有時才 attachShadow()；行為直接掛在現有標記上
Blocker
升級時重新渲染 shadow tree，會丟掉伺服器送來的 DOM 並造成閃爍（引用："do NOT re-render; that would discard the server's DOM and flash."）
Outcome
勝——把伺服器標記當成權威、原地接上事件，畫面不重繪（官方宣稱）

元件類別照客戶端渲染的習慣，在 constructor 裡無條件建 shadow root 並塞模板，伺服器辛苦送來的內容被整個換掉，畫面閃一下。改成「有就用、沒有才建」就沒事。

Support Declarative shadow DOM：Widely（2024-02-20）

mwg:prerendering-custom-elements:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

Who acts

Action + object
模組層建立一個 CSSStyleSheet 並 replaceSync()，每個實例的 shadow root 以 adoptedStyleSheets = [sheet] 共用
Blocker

清單裡上百張卡片，每張 shadow 裡都有同一段 <style>，DOM 裡就有上百份一樣的 CSS。共用一個建構式樣式表，數量再多也只解析一次。

mwg:shadow-dom · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 slotchange 與 assignedElements() 判斷 slot 有沒有內容 Correctionuntranslated Shadow DOM、模板與 slot · Wrong lifecycle assumptions
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
監聽 slot 的 slotchange，用 assignedElements() 取得實際分配到的元素，再決定例如是否切換 has-header
Blocker

Outcome
勝——slotchange 在分配改變（含第一次分配）時觸發，是子節點到達的可靠訊號（官方宣稱）

想在沒有標題時隱藏標題列，檢查預設 slot 有沒有節點，結果永遠有（換行與縮排），標題列一直在；在 connectedCallback 檢查又常常太早。改看 assignedElements() 並在 slotchange 時更新。

mwg:shadow-dom:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用繼承的自訂屬性做主題，不要用 :host-context() Correctionuntranslated 替 Web Components 加樣式 · 押注不會普及的功能
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
使用端在祖先上設 --card-bg 這類自訂屬性，元件內以 var(--card-bg, white) 讀取，取代 :host-context([theme="dark"])
Blocker
:host-context() 只有 Chromium 支援，Firefox 與 Safari 已拒絕實作，CSSWG 也傾向不再發展（引用："Firefox and Safari have declined to ship it and the CSSWG is leaning toward not developing it further — treat it as a Chromium-only dead end"）
Outcome
勝——自訂屬性可繼承、穿過 shadow 邊界，在每個瀏覽器都有效，兩邊也不互相綁死（官方宣稱）

元件用 :host-context([theme="dark"]) 偵測深色模式，在 Chrome 裡正常，換到 Firefox、Safari 元件就停在淺色。改成由頁面設定自訂屬性、元件讀取，主題在哪都會傳進去。

Support :host-context()（被取代的做法）：Limited availability，Supported by: Chrome 54, Edge 79；Unsupported in: Firefox, Safari。自訂屬性：指南原文 "works everywhere"，未附 Baseline 資料，記 Widely

mwg:styling-web-components · Upstream guide · Line file ·

公開的自訂屬性不要在 :host 上設值，用私有別名放預設 Correctionuntranslated 替 Web Components 加樣式 · Cascade overrides don't match intent
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
:host 上只定義私有別名 --_card-bg: var(--card-bg, white)，內部一律用 --_card-bg，公開的 --card-bg 保持未設定
Blocker
在 :host 上定義的自訂屬性，只能被直接指定宿主元素的規則覆寫，祖先上設的值傳不進來（引用："A custom property *defined* on :host can only be overridden by rules targeting the host element specifically."）
Outcome
勝——公開屬性沒被宿主截斷，可以從樹上任何一層繼承覆寫，預設值也只寫一次（官方宣稱）

在 :host 寫 --card-bg: white 當預設，頁面在 body 上設 --card-bg: navy 卻沒反應，因為宿主自己的宣告優先。改用私有別名，公開屬性就能從外面一路繼承進來。

Support 自訂屬性：指南原文 "works everywhere"，未附 Baseline 資料，記 Widely

mwg:styling-web-components:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

包住原生控制項時設 font: inherit Correctionuntranslated 替 Web Components 加樣式 · Cascade overrides don't match intent
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
shadow tree 內被包住的 <button>、<input>、<select> 設 font: inherit（通常再加 color: inherit）
Blocker
表單控制項的 font 來自 UA 樣式表、不繼承，被包住的控制項會顯示系統字型而不是頁面字型，這是最常見的遺漏（引用："The wrapped control therefore renders in the platform font, not the page font, until you set font: inherit"）
Outcome
勝——加上 font: inherit 後，控制項跟頁面字型一致（官方宣稱）

<my-button> 放進頁面，周圍文字都是品牌字型，只有按鈕是系統預設字。不是邊界擋住了，而是表單控制項本來就不繼承字型。

mwg:styling-web-components:3 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

:host 的樣式不會傳到內部控制項，用 ::part 或自訂屬性轉交 Correctionuntranslated 替 Web Components 加樣式 · 封裝邊界切斷關聯
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
內部控制項標上 part="submit" 讓使用端以 my-card::part(submit) 自由設樣式，或用一組自訂屬性傳固定的值；巢狀元件以 exportparts 往上轉出
Blocker
在 :host 上設 background、border、padding 只會作用在宿主的盒子，不會層疊進被包住的元素（引用："Setting background/border/padding on :host styles the host box, not the control inside it."）
Outcome
勝——::part 已 Widely，使用端不必知道內部結構就能針對特定元素設樣式（官方宣稱）

使用端想把按鈕底色改掉，在 my-button 上設 background，結果外面多了一圈色塊，裡面的按鈕還是原樣。開放 part 或自訂屬性才是有意的通道；part 只開幾個高階的，開太多會變成脆弱的 API。

mwg:styling-web-components:4 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

display: contents 讓宿主量不到尺寸，錨點定位會壞 Correctionuntranslated 替 Web Components 加樣式 · Layout depends on the wrong measurement
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
要把任意樣式傳給被包住的原生元素時，若用 display: contents 加 all: inherit，要知道它的代價；不適合時改用 ::part 或自訂屬性
Blocker
display: contents 的元素 getBoundingClientRect() 與 getClientRects() 都回傳 0，以宿主為錨點的定位因此壞掉；all: inherit 也會抹掉 UA 預設（引用："getBoundingClientRect() and getClientRects() return zero for display: contents elements, so anchor positioning against the host is broken."）
Outcome
部分改善——這是目前唯一能把任意樣式傳進原生元素的通用做法，但量測與錨點的代價仍在，只能依情況改用 part 或自訂屬性（官方宣稱）

為了讓 <my-button> 完全透明而把宿主設成 display: contents，樣式傳遞成功了，但掛在這顆按鈕上的提示框找不到位置，因為宿主沒有盒子。指南把它講成取捨，不是捷徑。

Support 不適用（指南未附 display: contents 的支援資料）

mwg:styling-web-components:5 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

元件要用 container query 時，先給 :host 一個區塊盒 Correctionuntranslated 替 Web Components 加樣式 · Layout depends on the wrong measurement
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
:host { display: block; container-type: inline-size; }，再用 @container 依元件自己的寬度調整版面
Blocker
自訂元素預設 display: inline，尺寸 containment 不作用在 inline 或 contents 元素上，容器查詢就失效（引用："custom elements default to display: inline and size containment does not apply to inline or contents elements"）
Outcome
勝——容器查詢已 Widely；宿主是區塊盒後，同一個元件放在側欄或全寬都會依自己的空間調整（官方宣稱）

在元件裡寫了 container-type 和 @container，怎麼調寬度都不變，因為宿主是 inline。補一行 display: block 就生效。

Support Container queries (size)：Widely（2023-02-14）

mwg:styling-web-components:6 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

包住真正的 <button>，不要重做原生語意 Correctionuntranslated Web Components 導覽 · Hand-built controls lose native behavior
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
只有需要封裝狀態、可腳本化的 API 或原生元素做不到的行為時才寫自訂元素；需要包裝時，把真正的 <button> 包在裡面，而不是用 <my-button> 重做焦點、啟動與 ARIA
Blocker
缺——指南說重做原生語意是最常見的誤用、只有沿用原生元素才能免費繼承可及性，但沒說重做版在哪個時刻出錯
Outcome
勝——沿用原生元素，焦點、啟動、可及性都由平台提供（官方宣稱）

為了換個外觀就從零寫一個按鈕元件，焦點、Enter／Space、角色都要自己補。指南的判準是：需要的是封裝狀態或可腳本化的 API，而不只是不同的樣子；只是變色的按鈕用 <button> 加 CSS 就好。

Support 不適用（本指南未附支援資料）

mwg:web-components · Upstream guide · Line file ·

shadow root 用 open 模式 Correctionuntranslated Web Components 導覽 · 封裝邊界切斷關聯
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
attachShadow({ mode: 'open' })，宣告式則用 shadowrootmode="open"
Blocker
mode: 'closed' 會讓頁面腳本、測試工具與會走訪 shadow tree 的自動無障礙檢查器都拿不到 element.shadowRoot（引用："mode: 'closed' hides element.shadowRoot from page scripts, test harnesses, and automated accessibility checkers that walk shadow trees."）
Outcome
勝——open 模式下工具與 hydrate 腳本都能進入 shadow tree（官方宣稱）

為了「封裝得更徹底」用了 closed，結果自動化無障礙檢查掃不到元件內部，測試也只能從外面猜。封裝靠的是 shadow 邊界本身，不是把 root 藏起來。

Support 不適用（本指南未附支援資料）

mwg:web-components:3 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 ZonedDateTime 的 since()／until() 算時間差，不用 Date 毫秒相減 New capabilityuntranslated 用 Temporal 計算事件時間差並格式化時長 · 時間模型失真
Unlock · brainstorm倒數、試用剩餘、「距離上次 X 已經 3 個月又 2 天」這類以曆法單位顯示的時間差可以直接算，不必載 moment／date-fns。Safari 要 polyfill
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
兩端點都做成 Temporal.ZonedDateTime（指定 IANA 時區），用 .since()／.until() 搭配 largestUnit、smallestUnit 算出 Temporal.Duration；比較時長用 Temporal.Duration.compare(d1, d2, { relativeTo })，到期與否看 .sign
Blocker
缺——指南只說舊 Date 只記毫秒，讓跨月計算、日光節約轉換與單位進位「容易出錯」，以前得靠手算或外部函式庫，沒指出哪個具體情境算錯（引用："The legacy Date object only tracks epoch milliseconds, making calendar-month arithmetic, Daylight Saving Time (DST) transitions, and unit balancing error-prone."）
Outcome
部分改善——Temporal 給出不受日光節約影響、可設定進位的時間差與依曆法比較；Safari 尚未支援，要條件載入約 15 KB 的 temporal-polyfill（官方宣稱）

試用剩幾天、訂閱何時到期、SLA 還剩多久，用毫秒相減再除以 86400000，遇到日光節約或大小月就差一點。ZonedDateTime 讓差值以曆法單位計算並依需求進位。注意預設 largestUnit 是 hour，要人看的拆解得自己指定 day 或 month。

Support Temporal：Limited availability。Supported by: Chrome 144, Edge 144, Firefox 139；Unsupported in: Safari

mwg:calculate-event-differentials:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

生日、鬧鐘這類日期用 Temporal.PlainDate／PlainTime，擋掉跨時區少一天 New capabilityuntranslated 用 Temporal 記錄與地點無關的資料和不完整日期 · 時間模型失真
Unlock · brainstorm生日提醒、每天 9 點的鬧鐘、店家營業時間可以存成「與地點無關」的值，跨時區的使用者、伺服器、同步裝置看到的都是同一天同一刻。Safari 要 polyfill
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
純日期用 Temporal.PlainDate.from()、牆上時間用 PlainTime、兩者合起來用 PlainDateTime，直接讀 .year、.month、.day，比較用 .equals()；非得交給 Date 時經由 Temporal.Instant 明確指定時區

使用者在台北填了生日，紐約的客服後台看到的是前一天。不是資料錯，而是 Date 本來就沒有「日曆上的某一天」這種概念。PlainDate 就是那一天本身，不需要選一個午夜時刻去代表它。

mwg:capture-location-agnostic-data · Upstream guide · Line file ·

信用卡到期、年度紀念日用 PlainYearMonth／PlainMonthDay，不要塞假的日 New capabilityuntranslated 用 Temporal 記錄與地點無關的資料和不完整日期 · 時間模型失真
Unlock · brainstorm訂閱扣款日、每年的紀念日提醒、帳單月份這類「不完整日期」可以照字面存與比較，月底扣款的規則（夾到月底或拒絕）寫成一個參數。Safari 要 polyfill
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
年月用 Temporal.PlainYearMonth、月日用 Temporal.PlainMonthDay（物件建構時明確給 calendar: 'iso8601'）；要做運算時先 .toPlainDate({ year })；每月循環用 .add({ months: 1 }) 搭配 overflow: 'constrain' 或 'reject'
Blocker
用 Date 表示只有月份的卡片到期日，得塞一個隨意的日子，造成溢位錯誤（引用："modeling partial dates (like a month-only card expiry) forces arbitrary placeholder days that cause overflow bugs."）
Outcome

卡片到期「2027-12」存成 12 月 31 日，或每月 31 號扣款，一到 2 月就溢到 3 月。PlainYearMonth 根本沒有日欄位，overflow 則把「1 月 31 日加一個月」的行為變成明確的選擇。

Support Temporal：Limited availability（同上）

mwg:capture-location-agnostic-data:3 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 ZonedDateTime 的 disambiguation 偵測日光節約跳過或重複的時刻 New capabilityuntranslated 用 Temporal 協調跨時區事件 · 時間模型失真
Unlock · brainstorm跨國團隊的排程工具可以在使用者選到「不存在」或「出現兩次」的時刻時當場提醒，並同時列出每位與會者的當地時間。Safari 要 polyfill
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
Temporal.ZonedDateTime.from() 建立有時區的時間；disambiguation: 'reject' 在時刻不存在或重複時丟 RangeError 以警告使用者，'compatible' 自動挑一個合理時間；.withTimeZone() 換算其他城市
Blocker
日光節約轉換時，某些時刻會被跳過或重複，舊 Date 很難安排這類跨時區事件（引用："especially around Daylight Saving Time (DST) transitions when hours can be skipped or repeated."）
Outcome

會議排在紐約 2025-03-09 02:30，那一刻因為日光節約根本不存在。'reject' 讓排程介面能直接提示「這個時間落在時鐘調整的空隙」，再用 withTimeZone 給東京與會者看對應時間。

Support Temporal：Limited availability。Supported by: Chrome 144, Edge 144, Firefox 139；Unsupported in: Safari

mwg:coordinate-global-events · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 Temporal.Instant 解析奈秒時間戳，擋掉同一毫秒內的事件排序錯亂 New capabilityuntranslated 用 Temporal.Instant 排序高精度時間戳 · 時間模型失真
Unlock · brainstorm在瀏覽器裡做的日誌檢視器、追蹤時間軸、遙測面板可以照伺服器記錄的奈秒精度排序並顯示「3μs 456ns」這種間隔，不用把時間戳當字串比。Safari 要 polyfill
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
伺服器或日誌給的 ISO 8601 時間戳用 Temporal.Instant.from() 解析，Temporal.Instant.compare() 當排序比較函式，.since() 算間隔、.equals() 判斷相同
Blocker
Date 只存毫秒，new Date()／Date.parse() 會默默截掉毫秒以下的位數，同一毫秒內的不同事件撞在一起、排序不穩定（引用："parsing those strings with new Date() or Date.parse() silently truncates sub-millisecond digits, causing distinct events within the same millisecond to collide and sort in an unstable order."）
Outcome
部分改善——Instant 保留完整奈秒值，排序確定、間隔精確；Safari 需 polyfill。瀏覽器自己的 Temporal.Now.instant() 仍被刻意降低解析度（官方宣稱）

分散式追蹤的三筆事件都在 12:00:00.000 這一毫秒內，用 Date 解析後時間完全相同，畫出來的順序每次都可能不一樣。Instant 保住後面的六位數，request.start、db.query、request.end 永遠按真實順序排。

Support Temporal：Limited availability。Supported by: Chrome 144, Edge 144, Firefox 139；Unsupported in: Safari

mwg:sort-high-precision-timestamps · Upstream guide · Line file ·

間隔保持 Temporal.Duration 再格式化，不要轉成奈秒數字 Correctionuntranslated 用 Temporal.Instant 排序高精度時間戳 · 時間模型失真
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object

Blocker

Outcome

奈秒時間戳費心保住了，最後為了顯示轉成一個大數字，長區間的尾數就悄悄錯了。相減 epochNanoseconds（BigInt）雖然精確，卻丟了單位結構，直接格式化 Duration 最省事。

Unlock · brainstorm行事曆或節日提醒可以讓使用者選自己的曆法，農曆生日、猶太節日每年自動換算到正確的公曆日期，不必內建曆法表。Safari 要 polyfill

mwg:support-global-calendar-systems · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 monthsInYear、monthCode，不要假設一年 12 個月 Correctionuntranslated 用 Temporal 支援各地曆法 · 時間模型失真
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
迴圈上限用 monthsInYear、每月天數用 daysInMonth；跨年識別同一個月用 monthCode；判斷最後一個月用 date.month === date.monthsInYear
Blocker
在陰陽合曆裡，閏年可能多出一整個閏月，不只多一天；寫死 12 個月或用數字月份跨年比對就會錯（引用："DO NOT assume inLeapYear === true implies the year is only one day longer. In lunisolar calendars, it may add a full leap month."）
Outcome
部分改善——照曆法的不變量寫，同一段程式在各曆法都正確；Safari 需 polyfill（官方宣稱）

月曆元件寫死 12 格，換成希伯來曆的閏年就少畫一個月；用 month === 6 找某個節日，閏月一插進來就指到錯的月。monthCode 是跨年穩定的月份名稱。

Support Temporal：Limited availability（同上）

mwg:support-global-calendar-systems:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

用 fetchLater() 的 activateAfter 把一段時間內的事件併成一個 beacon New capabilityuntranslated 防抖並批次送出分析事件 · Background sends are unreliable or excessive
Unlock · brainstorm自架的輕量分析、編輯器的自動草稿、閱讀進度同步，都能「攢一小段再送、關分頁也不掉」，不必自己拼 visibilitychange 加 sendBeacon。目前僅 Chromium，其他靠 polyfill
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
有分析資料就呼叫 fetchLater(url, { body, activateAfter, signal })；時間窗內又有新事件時，以 AbortController 取消前一個請求、帶著整個佇列重新排程；result.activated 為真時清空佇列
Blocker
缺——指南說這是在即時更新與減少 beacon 之間的最佳做法，且使用者在逾時前離開也能可靠送出，但沒說舊做法在哪個時刻出問題
Outcome
部分改善——支援的瀏覽器把時間窗內的事件併成一次送出，離開頁面時由瀏覽器保證送出；Firefox 與 Safari 不支援，改用 polyfill，差別是改在 visibilityState 變成 hidden 時送（官方宣稱）

每個點擊都打一次 beacon 太多，全部攢到離開頁面才送又不即時，還可能在關分頁時遺失。fetchLater() 把「10 秒內沒新事件就送，或使用者先離開就立刻送」交給瀏覽器排程。目前只有 Chromium 原生支援。

Support fetchLater：Limited availability。Supported by: Chrome 135, Edge 135；Unsupported in: Firefox, Safari

mwg:batch-analytics-events · Upstream guide · Line file ·

fetchLater() 有配額，要限制批次大小並包 try/catch Correctionuntranslated 防抖並批次送出分析事件 · Side effects of a new feature
Who acts
開發者（或寫程式的代理）
Action + object
設定佇列上限（例如 100 筆）以免超過每個來源約 64KB 的配額，呼叫包在 try/catch 裡；body 不用 ReadableStream
Blocker
批次太大會超出 fetchLater() 的配額而丟出錯誤；用 ReadableStream 當 body 也會出錯（引用："DO check to ensure you do not batch too many events together and exceed the fetchLater() quota (currently roughly 64KB per origin)."）
Outcome
部分改善——限制大小並捕捉錯誤後，排程不會因配額而中斷；整體仍受 fetchLater 的支援度限制（官方宣稱）

佇列無限累積，某次重新排程時超過配額直接拋錯，後面的事件再也沒排上。加上上限與 try/catch，最壞情況是重置佇列而不是整條管線停擺。

Support fetchLater：Limited availability（同上）

mwg:batch-analytics-events:2 · Upstream guide · Line file ·

Slice long loops with scheduler.yield() so clicks don't wait for the loop to finish New capabilityPartialLimited Break up long tasks · Heavy work blocks the main thread
Unlock · brainstormParse a large CSV, build a full-text index or batch-process photos in the browser while the UI still scrolls and a Cancel button still works, without moving to a Worker or freezing the page. Safari falls back to setTimeout.
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Give an async long loop a 50ms time budget; once it is exceeded, await scheduler.yield() to hand the main thread back to the browser, then reset the budget and continue
Blocker
Heavy computation or long loops hold the main thread, event handlers are delayed and INP suffers (quote: "Heavy computations or long loops can block the main thread, delaying event handlers and causing poor Interaction to Next Paint (INP) responsiveness.")
Outcome
Partial — Supporting browsers handle user input and rendering between slices; Safari lacks support, so a setTimeout(resolve, 0) fallback is mandatory to yield the main thread (official claim)

Process a whole large array in one go and every click waits until the loop ends. Yielding every 50ms lets input and painting cut in. The guide doesn't say how the setTimeout fallback falls short of scheduler.yield(), only that it must exist.

Support Scheduler API: Limited (Chrome 129, Edge 129, Firefox 142; not Safari)

mwg:break-up-long-tasks · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Use visibility-state performance entries to count real viewing time, excluding background tabs New capabilityStalemateLimited Calculate total foreground time · Performance measurement is skewed or blind
Unlock · brainstormA blog or online course can count the minutes each article was actually looked at and use that to pick articles worth rewriting, without inflation from tabs left open. Chromium only for now.
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Read the whole visibility history with performance.getEntriesByType('visibility-state') and sum each visible span up to the next state change
Blocker
Traditional time-on-page also counts time the page spent in the background (quote: "Traditional metrics like time-on-page often incorrectly include time spent with the page in the background.")
Outcome
Stalemate — Chrome/Edge can compute foreground time from the performance timeline at any moment; Firefox and Safari return an empty array and the fallback is performance.now(), an upper bound that still includes background time (official claim)

A user opens a tab and switches away, and time-on-page keeps ticking, inflating engagement. Visibility entries record every switch on the timeline, so there is no need to accumulate visibilitychange events by hand. Only Chromium has them; other browsers still get the old number with background time included.

Support Page visibility state performance entries: Limited (Chrome 115, Edge 115; not Firefox or Safari)

mwg:calculate-total-foreground-time · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Use top-level await to load a polyfill only when the feature is missing, and make downstream modules wait for it New capabilityPartialNewly Conditional async dependencies · Loading more than is needed
Unlock · brainstormSmall tools using newer features like popover or anchor positioning can load a polyfill only for older browsers, with zero extra download in newer ones and no 'wait for the polyfill' callbacks scattered through the main code.
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Feature-detect in a dedicated module (for example 'popover' in HTMLElement.prototype) and await import() the polyfill when it's missing; the entry module statically imports that module before using the feature
Blocker
Missing: the guide only says this encapsulates conditional loading in one module and keeps downstream code from running until the dependency is ready; it doesn't say where the old approach goes wrong (quote: "effectively preventing downstream consumer modules from executing until the dependency is fully loaded and ready.")
Outcome
Partial — Top-level await has been Baseline only since 2026-09-14 (Safari 27 fixed its module loader); on Safari 15–26.6 it is safe as long as you follow the single-entry pattern (official claim)

Polyfills or heavy secondary libraries are only needed by browsers that lack the feature. Wrapped in a top-level-await module, supporting browsers skip the download and others are guaranteed to have it loaded before use. The guide doesn't describe what breaks without this pattern.

Support Top-level await: Newly (2026-09-14; Chrome 89, Edge 89, Firefox 89, Safari 27)

mwg:conditional-async-dependencies · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Top-level await only suspends the importing parent, not sibling imports CorrectionPartialNewly Conditional async dependencies · Wrong lifecycle assumptions
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Import the top-level-await module from a single entry point; when the entry delegates to other app modules, chain await import('./polyfill.js'); await import('./app.js'); instead of two sibling static imports, and don't let several sibling modules import it at once
Blocker
Sibling static imports let app.js run before the polyfill has loaded; Safari 15–26.6 fails outright when several modules import a top-level-await module at the same time (quote: "top-level await only suspends the parent module that imports it—not sibling static imports—so ./app.js will evaluate concurrently in all browsers before the polyfill finishes loading.")
Outcome
Partial — With a single entry or chained dynamic imports, app.js is always fetched and run after the polyfill, and the old Safari bug isn't triggered (official claim)

The intuitive version is two imports in the entry file, which looks ordered, but sibling modules still evaluate concurrently and showPopover() gets called before the polyfill arrives. Chaining dynamic imports is what actually guarantees the order.

Support Top-level await: Newly (same as above); the Safari 15–26.6 concurrent-import bug is fixed in Safari 27

mwg:conditional-async-dependencies:2 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Use content-visibility: auto so heavy below-the-fold blocks are laid out and painted only when scrolled near New capabilityPartialNewly Defer rendering heavy content · Work spent on what nobody can see
Unlock · brainstormPut an entire manual, a whole year of weekly notes or thousands of comments on one page and still have it open instantly, without pagination or virtual scrolling for performance's sake.
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Add content-visibility: auto to large blocks with deep DOM that are confirmed below the initial fold (infinite-feed cards, comment threads, heavy footer sections)
Blocker
Content-heavy pages (infinite scroll, complex dashboards, long articles) render slowly at first and respond sluggishly (quote: "Web pages with extensive content—such as infinite scrolls, complex dashboards, or dense articles can suffer from slow initial rendering and sluggish interactions.")
Outcome
Partial — The browser defers layout and paint for off-screen blocks until they are scrolled near; unsupported browsers ignore the property and render normally, just without the performance gain (official claim)

Hundreds of cards get laid out and painted on load, so the first screen is slow and clicks lag. With content-visibility: auto, off-screen blocks are skipped first and computed only when the user scrolls close.

Support content-visibility: Newly (2025-09-15; Chrome 108, Edge 108, Firefox 130, Safari 26)

mwg:defer-rendering-heavy-content · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Always pair content-visibility: auto with contain-intrinsic-size CorrectionPartialNewly Defer rendering heavy content · Layout unexpectedly overflows or shifts
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Give every content-visibility: auto block an estimated size such as contain-intrinsic-size: auto none auto 150px; auto lets the browser remember the real rendered size
Blocker
Without an estimate, off-screen blocks collapse to 0px height, so layout shifts badly and the scrollbar jumps while scrolling (quote: "Failure to do so forces the browser to collapse the element to a 0px height when off-screen, causing severe layout shifting and scrollbar jumping as the user scrolls.")
Outcome
Partial — With a placeholder size, scrolling no longer jumps, and blocks that have rendered keep their remembered size after leaving the screen (official claim)

Add only content-visibility: auto and every block flips between 0 and its real height as it enters and leaves the screen, so the scrollbar length keeps changing. The estimate keeps them occupying space while skipped.

Support content-visibility: Newly (same as above)

mwg:defer-rendering-heavy-content:2 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Don't put content-visibility: auto on above-the-fold elements CorrectionPartialNewly Defer rendering heavy content · Side effects of a new feature
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Apply content-visibility: auto only to blocks outside the initial viewport, never to elements within it
Blocker
On above-the-fold elements the browser must evaluate visibility boundaries before rendering, which delays critical page load (quote: "Doing so forces the browser to evaluate visibility boundaries before rendering, which paradoxically delays critical page load performance.")
Outcome
Partial — Applied only below the fold, the first screen renders immediately as usual (official claim)

Adding the same class to every section for convenience means the first screen also goes through a visibility check, so the part that most needs to be fast gets slower.

Support content-visibility: Newly (same as above)

mwg:defer-rendering-heavy-content:3 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Use content-visibility: hidden to hide content while keeping its rendering state, so toggling is instant New capabilityPartialNewly Defer rendering heavy content · Work spent on what nobody can see
Unlock · brainstormA data table's 'show all rows' or a settings page's tabs can be built up front and kept hidden at no cost, appearing instantly on click. For a concrete statement of the blocker see mwg:faster-spa-view-transitions.
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Set content-visibility: hidden on initially hidden heavy blocks (extra rows of a large data table, switchable views) and change it to visible or auto to reveal; wrap it in @supports (content-visibility: hidden) with display: none as the default
Blocker
Missing: the guide only says it is better than display: none, without saying what concretely goes wrong with display: none when toggling (quote: "superior to display: none")
Outcome
Partial — Hidden content skips layout but keeps its state, so toggling is near-instant without structural shifts; unsupported browsers show hidden elements in full, so fall back to display: none via @supports (official claim)

Switching back and forth between views recomputes each one every time it reappears. With content-visibility: hidden, nothing is laid out or painted while hidden, and the rendered result is kept and reused on reveal.

Support content-visibility: Newly (same as above)

mwg:defer-rendering-heavy-content:4 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

content-visibility can take content out of the accessibility tree and find-in-page CorrectionPartialNewly Defer rendering heavy content · Hiding or disabling locks people out
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Use hidden="until-found" instead of content-visibility: hidden for content that must stay searchable while hidden; when using content-visibility: auto, walk through the page with the keyboard alone to confirm off-screen nodes are still reachable in order
Blocker
content-visibility: hidden removes the element and its children from the accessibility tree and find-in-page; with auto, off-screen nodes may also be missing from the accessibility tree or sequential navigation in some assistive-tech setups until focus moves inside (quote: "In certain assistive technology configurations, off-screen nodes utilizing content-visibility: auto may be excluded from the accessibility tree or sequential navigation routes until focus is forcibly moved inside them.")
Outcome
Partial — For hidden, switching to hidden="until-found" keeps find-in-page working; for auto, the guide only requires keyboard testing and offers no fix (official claim)

Swap collapsed content to content-visibility: hidden for performance and Ctrl+F can no longer find the text inside. Off-screen auto blocks may also be unreachable in order for some screen reader users.

Support content-visibility: Newly (same as above); hidden="until-found" support is not listed in this guide (see search-hidden-content)

mwg:defer-rendering-heavy-content:5 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Use scrollend instead of debouncing to guess when scrolling is over New capabilityPartialNewly Defer work until scroll ends · Guessing when something has finished
Unlock · brainstormWork like 'remember reading position by chapter' or 'load the high-res version of the image you stopped on' can run once when the user really stops, instead of several times during momentum scrolling.
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Listen for scrollend on the scroll container or document and put content fetching, layout recalculation and analytics beacons in that callback
Blocker
Debouncing scroll with setTimeout to guess the end is unreliable and can fire while the user is still scrolling (quote: "However, these debounced functions are notoriously unreliable. They may trigger while the user’s is still scrolling.")
Outcome
Partial — The browser fires scrollend only once scrolling has truly rested, transitions are done and touch is released, including visual-viewport zoom and scroll; unsupported browsers fall back to a debounce that dispatches its own scrollend (official claim)

A finger still slowly dragging, or momentum scrolling not yet stopped, and the 100ms debounce has already fired, starting fetches and layout mid-scroll. scrollend lets the browser decide the real end point, with no wait time to guess.

Support scrollend: Newly (2025-12-12; Chrome 114, Edge 114, Firefox 109, Safari 26.2)

mwg:defer-work-until-scroll-ends · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Keep heavy work out of scroll callbacks CorrectionPartialNewly Defer work until scroll ends · Heavy work blocks the main thread
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Limit scroll callbacks to lightweight live feedback and move layout-dependent updates and data fetching to scrollend
Blocker
Layout recalculation, analytics beacons and DOM updates during scrolling saturate the main thread, causing dropped frames and layout thrashing (quote: "Executing heavy tasks—such as layout recalculations, analytics data beacons tracking, or dynamic DOM updates—during scrolling can saturate the main thread, resulting in dropped frames and layout thrashing.")
Outcome
Partial — Only light work remains during scrolling and heavy work runs once on scrollend; without scrollend support it is emulated with a debounce (official claim)

Measuring layout and sending a beacon on every scroll event makes scrolling stutter. Moving that work to after scrolling ends keeps the scroll itself smooth.

Support scrollend: Newly (same as above)

mwg:defer-work-until-scroll-ends:2 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Use image-set() so CSS background images also pick the smallest file by format and resolution CorrectionWinWidely Deliver optimized decorative images · Loading more than is needed
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
In background-image, mask-image or content, use image-set() to list AVIF/WebP/JPEG at 1x and 2x, ordered from most to least optimized format
Blocker
Missing: the guide only says this improves perceived performance and the browser picks the smallest suitable image; it doesn't say where a single url() goes wrong (quote: "Delivering optimized decorative images via CSS improves perceived performance without sacrificing visual quality.")
Outcome
Win — The browser picks the smallest image matching supported formats and device pixel density; for older browsers add a plain background-image before it as fallback (official claim)

Decorative CSS images used to allow only one URL, serving neither high-DPI screens nor new formats. image-set() gives CSS images a srcset-like choice. The guide doesn't describe the concrete cost of a single URL.

mwg:deliver-optimized-decorative-images · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Don't leave a likely LCP image only inside CSS image-set() CorrectionWinWidely Deliver optimized decorative images · Loading priority is misplaced
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Use an HTML <img> or <picture> for LCP candidates such as hero banners, or additionally preload them with <link rel=preload> and a media attribute
Blocker
Images referenced via CSS image-set() are invisible to the preload scanner, delaying their load and hurting LCP (quote: "images referenced in CSS via image-set() are not discoverable by the browser's preload scanner. This can significantly delay image loading and harm LCP.")
Outcome
Win — In HTML or preloaded, the preload scanner discovers the image while parsing HTML and starts downloading it (official claim)

With the hero image as a CSS background, the browser only learns it needs it after the stylesheet downloads and applies, pushing LCP back. Moving it into HTML or a preload starts the download at the earliest point.

Support image-set(): Widely (same as above); no support data listed for <img>, <picture> or <link rel=preload>

mwg:deliver-optimized-decorative-images:2 · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Use fetch() priority: 'low' so analytics and prefetches give way to data the user is waiting for New capabilityPartialNewly Deprioritize background fetches · Loading priority is misplaced
Unlock · brainstormPrefetch the next chapter, next page or thumbnails freely in the background without fear of slowing the user's current search or submit; 'guess the next step' interfaces like readers and photo albums get cheaper.
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Add priority: 'low' to the fetch() options for analytics, logging, background sync and prefetches of data that might be needed later; leave it off for requests critical to the experience
Blocker
Simultaneous requests compete for the same bandwidth, slowing the user-initiated critical ones (quote: "When a page performs multiple simultaneous network requests, they often compete for the same bandwidth.")
Outcome
Partial — Low-priority requests no longer slow critical ones; unsupported browsers ignore the option and use default priority, no fallback needed (official claim)

The user clicks, and the data the screen needs shares the line with analytics events and background sync sent at the same time. Lowering background requests lets the browser give bandwidth to the one the user is waiting on.

Support Fetch priority: Newly (2024-10-29; Chrome 103, Edge 103, Firefox 132, Safari 17.2)

mwg:deprioritize-background-fetches · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Use visibility-state performance entries to tell whether the page loaded in a background tab New capabilityStalemateLimited Detect initial visibility state · Performance measurement is skewed or blind
Unlock · brainstormA self-hosted RUM can tag background-opened visits automatically and report them separately, so median load times aren't inflated by people who middle-click links into new tabs. Chromium only for now.
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Read performance.getEntriesByType('visibility-state'); if the first entry is hidden the page loaded in the background, and the first hidden entry's startTime is when it went to the background; filter such pages out of performance analytics
Blocker
Reading document.visibilityState when the script runs is racy: a page opened in the background and foregrounded before the script finished reads visible and is misjudged as a foreground load (quote: "When your script finally runs, document.visibilityState will synchronously read as 'visible', and you will incorrectly assume the page was loaded in the foreground, completely missing its initial hidden state.")
Outcome
Stalemate — Chrome/Edge's timeline keeps the full visibility history no matter when the script runs; Firefox and Safari can only fall back to document.visibilityState, which the guide calls "highly inaccurate" for detecting initial background state (official claim)

Pages opened in background tabs render late and stretch metrics like FCP, skewing the stats. Async analytics scripts often run after the user has switched back, so checking only the current state misses it. Only Chromium gets the right answer.

Support Page visibility state performance entries: Limited (Chrome 115, Edge 115; not Firefox or Safari)

mwg:detect-initial-visibility-state · Upstream guide · Line file · v1.0.0

Use contentvisibilityautostatechange to pause animations and polling while a component isn't rendered New capabilityPartialNewly Efficient background processing · Work spent on what nobody can see
Unlock · brainstormPut many live canvases on one page (several stock charts, physics simulations, WebGL previews) with only the ones near the viewport running and the rest paused automatically, so the laptop fan stays quiet.
Who acts
A developer (or a coding agent)
Action + object
Give heavy components content-visibility: auto and contain-intrinsic-size, and listen for contentvisibilityautostatechange: when event.skipped is true stop canvas animation, WebGL rendering and high-frequency WebSocket polling, otherwise resume
Blocker

Outcome
Partial — The event follows the browser's rendering lifecycle and resumes when the element enters the pre-render margin, so content is ready when it appears; in unsupported browsers the event never fires and work keeps running, roughly emulated with IntersectionObserver (official claim)

Simulations, charts and live data panels scrolled off-screen keep computing every frame. Tied to this event, they stop when the browser decides not to render them and restart when it prepares to. Unlike IntersectionObserver, it maps to render timing rather than geometric visibility.

Action + object
Attach the listener directly to the element with content-visibility: auto, or add { capture: true } when delegating from a parent
Blocker
